    "addedLines": [
      "- claude: Catch-all for any task that doesn't fit a more specific agent. FleetView's default when no agent name is typed. (Tools: *)",
      "- Explore: Fast read-only search agent for locating code. Use it to find files by pattern (eg. \"src/components/**/*.tsx\"), grep for symbols or keywords (eg. \"API endpoints\"), or answer \"where is X defined / which files reference Y.\" Do NOT use it for code review, design-doc auditing, cross-file consistency checks, or open-ended analysis — it reads excerpts rather than whole files and will miss content past its read window. When calling, specify search breadth: \"quick\" for a single targeted lookup, \"medium\" for moderate exploration, or \"very thorough\" to search across multiple locations and naming conventions. (Tools: All tools except Agent, ExitPlanMode, Edit, Write, NotebookEdit)",
      "- general-purpose: General-purpose agent for researching complex questions, searching for code, and executing multi-step tasks. When you are searching for a keyword or file and are not confident that you will find the right match in the first few tries use this agent to perform the search for you. (Tools: *)",
      "- Plan: Software architect agent for designing implementation plans. Use this when you need to plan the implementation strategy for a task. Returns step-by-step plans, identifies critical files, and considers architectural trade-offs. (Tools: All tools except Agent, ExitPlanMode, Edit, Write, NotebookEdit)",
      "- statusline-setup: Use this agent to configure the user's Claude Code status line setting. (Tools: Read, Edit)"
    ],

{
  "parentUuid": "a46ea3ae-e61e-4cef-9dc1-a5b20fb698a9",

  "attachment": {
    "type": "skill_listing",
    "content": "- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch",

\"random_ign_f1\": 0.439677}, \"notes\": \"CED reranker (N=8, no training) rel_f1=0.4864, 超过 SFT greedy (+8.07pp) 和 RSFT 3-seed mean (+2.68pp)。Oracle 上界 rel_f1=0.6237 (+21.8pp vs SFT)，CED reranker 实现了 oracle ceiling 的 37% (0.0807/0.218)。CED-F1 Spearman r=0.3235 (p≈0)，相关性显著但中等——说明 CED 不是完美的 proxy 但有信号。Random baseline rel_f1=0.4538 说明 sampling diversity 本身就有 +4.8pp 收益，CED 在此基础上再加 +3.3pp。EDCR 下降 (0.6156 vs SFT 0.7118)，N=8 sampling 引入更多 distractor citations。推理耗时 5.8h (N=8 生成主导)。结论：CED 作为 inference-time reranker 有明确论文价值——零训练成本超越 RSFT，且 oracle ceiling 表明更好的 reward model 还有大幅空间。\"}, <details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_01TcWr9UK34Q37nBtbyp19Km`</summary>

```text
1	---
2	name: resource-saturation
3	description: "当你准备向 Director 汇报前、收到 cron/worker 回调、审批通过准备开跑、怀疑 Worker 池不够饱和时,立即使用本 skill。四部分:A) GPU 分配率(分配了没跑=失职) B) GPU 效率 C) 非 GPU 任务并行(Worker 池<2=失职) D) 汇报必须带 [资源] 行。"
4	always: false
5	roles:
6	  - director
7	  - agent
8	---
9	
10	# 资源饱和度自检（硬约束）
11	
12	## A. GPU 分配率检查
13	
14	调 `get_project_resource_usage(project)` 看:
15	- 已分配给本项目的 GPU 总数和具体卡号
16	- 实际在跑任务的 GPU 数和具体卡号（交叉 registry RUNNING + nvidia-smi 进程）
17	- 分配了但没跑的 GPU 清单 ⚠️
18	
19	任何"分配了但没跑"的 GPU = 失职。必须立即:
20	- 派 Worker 铺 dry-run / 下一批实验代码准备
21	- 铺 ablation（从 experiment-matrix 空白格子里挑）
22	- 确实无实验可铺 → 向 Director 汇报"暂无可铺实验"+ 建议下一步方向(扩 ablation 维度 / 加 baseline / 加 benchmark)。**不要建议归还 GPU**——项目级 GPU 归还只在归档时由 `archive_project` 自动触发, 不是 Director 中途的动作
23	
24	**单实验不占多卡**: 分配 4 卡但实验只用 1 卡 → 另 3 卡必须并行跑其他实验,不能空占。
25	
26	## B. GPU 效率检查
27	
28	调 `get_gpu_health(server)`:
29	- 空闲 GPU（util < 10% 持续 > 5 min）→ 立即铺任务
30	- 低利用率（util < 50% 持续 > 15 min）→ spawn_worker 诊断（batch / IO / 同步）
31	- OOM 风险（显存 > 90% 持续 10 min）→ 主动降 batch / 调优
32	
33	## C. 非 GPU 任务并行
34	
35	GPU 在跑 ≠ 你空闲。以下 CPU / 网络任务**必须**派独立 Worker 并行:
36	- 下一批实验的数据集下载 / 预处理 / fingerprint
37	- 下一批实验的代码准备 / dry-run / 环境配置
38	- 论文文献调研 / related work 检索
39	- 已完成实验的 log 深度分析 / metric 聚合
40	- 论文章节起草
41	- 代码 review / refactor
42	
43	**硬约束**: 任一时刻 Worker 池 < 2 个 = 失职（除非真的全部下游阻塞在等 GPU,这时应当向 Director 申请扩 GPU）。
44	
45	## D. 汇报格式
46	
47	向 Director 汇报时开头必须带资源声明行:
48	
49	    [资源] GPU 分配 N / 实际在跑 M | Worker 池 K (其中 GPU 任务 A / CPU 任务 B)
50	
```

For cuda:1, plan_002 test set evaluation is a priority—it's a P0 task that depends on plan_001 which is already complete. I should spawn this task now. I need to resolve the resource allocation issue—cuda:1 can't stay idle. The most practical approach is to run CED scoring on cuda:0 (about 1 hour), then follow up with RSFT training on cuda:1 once that completes, which gives me a clear pipeline for both GPUs. <details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
The previous plan_002 test eval failed because it was in RUNNING state with no cron and no server/pid info (orphan process). The command itself looks correct though. It was using `sft_adapter` pointing to the RSFT checkpoint, but looking at the plan_007_redocred_rsft_eval_r5 command which succeeded, it uses 3 parameters: `--model_path`, `--base_model`, and `--sft_adapter`. The test eval only uses `--model_path` and `--sft_adapter`. This might be wrong.


Actually, I see the issue now—the successful run loads the RSFT checkpoint as the model_path and applies the SFT adapter on top, while the failed test eval has them reversed, using the base model as model_path and the RSFT checkpoint as the adapter instead. I'm setting up two parallel workers to maximize GPU utilization: one handling CED scoring for the 1.7B candidates on cuda:0, and another running the plan_002 test evaluation with the corrected 3-parameter loading approach on cuda:1. I'm pausing briefly on whether plan_002 needs Director approval since it involves test set evaluation, but the ROADMAP already lists it as a task.
```

I've completed the main workflow steps—reading the registry, reconciling crons, identifying the successful completion of plan_006, and spawning the next batch of workers. Now I'm noting the key findings in memory.md: the 1.7B model achieved strong diversity scores at T=0.7, and there's a clear correlation between truncation rates and model size that's worth tracking.

The real insight to capture isn't just the metrics themselves, but the risk that the 10.4% truncation in smaller model candidates could skew CED scoring downward if the scoring mechanism doesn't handle incomplete evidence well—that's the non-derivable concern I should document.

Scoring\n\n```bash\nsource /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base && \\\ncd /root/autodl-tmp && \\\nCUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=1 HF_HOME=/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache \\\npython -m freige.training.rsft_score_filter \\\n    --input_dir /root/autodl-tmp/freige/outputs/rsft_candidates_8b \\\n    --output_path /root/autodl-tmp/freige/outputs/rsft_scored_8b \\\n    --nli_model_path cross-encoder/nli-deberta-v3-base \\\n    --nli_device cuda:0 \\\n    --scoring_mode ced --tau 0.5 \\\n    --selection_strategy top_k --top_k 3\n```\n\n### LLaMA-3.1-8B CED Scoring\n\n```bash\nsource /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base && \\\ncd /root/autodl-tmp && \\\nCUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=2 HF_HOME=/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache \\\npython -m freige.training.rsft_score_filter \\\n    --input_dir /root/autodl-tmp/rsft_candidates_llama_3_1_8b \\\n    --output_path /root/autodl-tmp/rsft_scored_llama_3_1_8b \\\n    --nli_model_path cross-encoder/nli-deberta-v3-base \\\n    --nli_device cuda:0 \\\n    --scoring_mode ced --tau 0.5 \\\n    --selection_strategy top_k --top_k 3\n```\n\n### 备选: flat_nli 模式（作为对比实验）\n\n将 `--scoring_mode ced` 替换为 `--scoring_mode flat_nli`，输出路径改为 `*_flat_nli`。\n\n## GPU 需求\n\n- CED scoring 使用 DeBERTa-v3-base NLI 模型，~400MB 显存，**任意一张卡即可**\n- 3 个 scoring 作业可在不同 GPU 上并行跑，不影响进行中的 generation\n- 计算量主要在 CPU 侧\n\n## 历史 4B Scoring 产出\n\n已有多轮 4B scoring 结果供参考：\n- `/root/autodl-tmp/rsft_scored_s0~s3/` — 分 split 的中间结果\n- `/root/autodl-tmp/rsft_filtered_topk3/` — top_k=3, 21728→8037 records (37.0%), 2691/2716 docs\n- `/root/autodl-tmp/rsft_filtered_flat_nli/` — flat_nli 模式对比\n- `/root/autodl-tmp/rsft_filtered_topk3_ced_filtered/` — 额外 CED 过滤\n\n## Evidence Tax Scaling Pattern (2026-05-16)\n\n**核心发现**：evidence tax 不是单调的，呈非线性 pattern：\n- 1.7B: +6.2pp benefit（公平子集分析，442 docs 无截断）— evidence 起隐式正则化作用，抑制过度预测\n- 4B: -7.9pp cost — 标准 evidence generation 负担\n- 8B Qwen: ≈0 — 模型容量足够 absorb\n- 8B LLaMA: -0.7pp — 跨家族确认大模型趋势\n\n**机制解释**：1.7B 模型容量不足时，evidence 约束减少幻觉（no-evi 输出 4513 triples vs with-evi 3991），precision 差 9.4pp。中等模型有足够容量生成正确 relations 但 evidence 成为额外负担。大模型容量冗余足以覆盖 evidence 生成。\n\n## Cross-Dataset Transfer Insight (2026-05-17)\n\n**Re-DocRED RSFT eval (plan_007_r5 成功, 3-param loading)**:\n- rel_f1: 0.4234 (DocRED 0.4655, -4.2pp)\n- evi_f1: 0.8182 (DocRED 0.8087, +1pp)\n\n**分析**: evidence quality 跨数据集迁移良好（甚至微升），rel_f1 下降主要因为 Re-DocRED 标注更全面（fewer false negatives = 更严格的评估标准）。这暗示论文可以 claim \"CED-RSFT 的 evidence grounding 能力是 dataset-agnostic 的\"，但 relation extraction 本身有 domain gap。\n\n**注意**: 1.7B 10.4% 截断率可能导致 CED scoring 时 incomplete evidence 得分偏低。如果 CED scoring 后高质量样本数量不足（远低于 4B 的 8037 条），需要降低 f1_threshold 或增大 top_k。\n\n## rsft_generate CLI 正确参数 (2026-05-16)\n\n**模块**: `freige.training.rsft_generate`（不是 freige.rsft.generate_candidates）\n\n正确参数名：\n- `--base_model`（不是 --model_path）\n- `--num_generations`（不是 --n_candidates）\n- 不加 --no-quantize（默认不量化）\n- `--data_path` 指向文件（如 train_annotated.json），不是目录\n- `--start_idx N` 支持断点续传\n\n## inference.py 正确参数（RSFT eval 三层加载）(2026-05-16)\n\n**模块**: `freige.eval.inference`\n\nRSFT 模型 eval 需要三个模型参数：\n- `--base_model`: 基础模型路径（如 `/root/autodl-tmp/models/Qwen3-4B`）\n- `--sft_adapter`: SFT adapter 路径（如 `/root/autodl-tmp/sft_output`）\n- `--model_path`: RSFT checkpoint 路径（如 `/root/autodl-tmp/rsft_output_ced_s43/checkpoint-510`）\n\n**加载顺序**: base_model → merge SFT adapter → load RSFT LoRA on top\n\n### Re-DocRED RSFT eval r4 命令（Director 确认）\n```bash\nbash -c \"source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base && cd /root/autodl-tmp && CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=1 HF_HOME=/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache HF_HUB_OFFLINE=1 TRANSFORMERS_OFFLINE=1 python -m freige.eval.inference --model_path /root/autodl-tmp/rsft_output_ced_s43/checkpoint-510 --base_model /root/autodl-tmp/models/Qwen3-4B --sft_adapter /root/autodl-tmp/sft_output --data_path /root/autodl-tmp/data/re-docred --split dev --batch_size 4 --max_new_tokens 1024 --seed 42 --no-quantize --output_dir /root/autodl-tmp/eval_results/plan_007_redocred_rsft_eval_r4\"\n```\n\n###
